### Organism names, ranks and experimental roles

evidence: supported. Targets: CultureMech:007312.

Lactococcus lactis NCDO 2118 and IL 1403 have separate exact strain mappings and experimental roles; their omission and sustained-growth results are not pooled. Actual NCBI ranks retained. Notes do not promote unresolved exact-recipe associations into native growth.

### Scoped edits and export boundary

evidence: supported. Targets: CultureMech:007312.

Only bibliography/qualified notes and one history event added. All prior fields and original bytes recoverable. No parent/child reassignment, graph edges or pages published.

## Findings

### fang-formulation: Fang and Deshpande formulas and derivative assignments unresolved

major / open / confirmed; issue key: defined-media-citations-fang-formulation.

Six imported recipes differ by base and amino-acid additions. Summary does not enumerate complete recipes or doses; the Deshpande base protocol is uninspected. Strain-specific growth factors remain unresolved.

### rdm-salinity: RDM salinity and RDM/SDM assay scopes need full-source evidence

major / open / confirmed; issue key: defined-media-citations-rdm-salinity.

The abstract separates RDM and SDM but does not supply the current RDM formulation or NaCl perturbation doses. Deletion-mutant and vitamin/nitrogen/sulfur outcomes are not unqualified R1 growth on every RDM child.

### lactococcus-formulation: MCD/MS formulas and omission versus sustained-growth scope unresolved

major / open / confirmed; issue key: defined-media-citations-lactococcus-formulation.

Amino-acid sets resemble local records, but the abstract does not establish their labels, doses, DL forms, MS10 glutamine identity or MS10R riboflavin variant. Ammonium omission is a study condition, not a parent edit.

### author-spelling: Cocaign-Bousquet last-author spelling discrepancy remains

minor / open / confirmed; issue key: defined-media-citations-author-spelling.

Publisher/Crossref metadata uses Loublere while the previously inspected Nordkvist bibliography uses Loubiere. Publication identity is verified; citation notes disclose the difference without silent correction.

### cache-portability: Reference title check depends on a local markup-normalized cache

minor / open / confirmed; issue key: defined-media-citations-cache-portability.

Five native checks failed because fetched title metadata included literal italic HTML tags. Verified markup-only conversion resolved the checks without changing recipe citations. A fresh cache may reproduce this formatting failure.

## Recommended Actions And Acceptance Checks

### resolve-fang-formulation

Fang and Deshpande formulas and derivative assignments unresolved

- Inspect authorized methods and cited base protocol; resolve ingredient identities, supplements and derivative-specific assay assignments before curation.

### resolve-rdm-salinity

RDM salinity and RDM/SDM assay scopes need full-source evidence

- Inspect complete methods/tables for each RDM owner and retain existing child scopes before adding concentration or growth claims.

### resolve-lactococcus-formulation

MCD/MS formulas and omission versus sustained-growth scope unresolved

- Inspect primary formulation tables and strain-specific repeated-culture conditions before assigning exact variants or quantities.

### resolve-author-spelling

Cocaign-Bousquet last-author spelling discrepancy remains

- Resolve the name from authoritative article/correction metadata, preserving which version supplied each spelling.

### resolve-cache-portability

Reference title check depends on a local markup-normalized cache

- Reproduce via preserved raw/deposited metadata and cache history; address generic title-markup normalization only in separately scoped validator work.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- Partial scientific self-review; all 383 amounts enumerated but not newly certified.
- Available sources are abstracts, not full articles. Organism inventory is limited to inspected medium-relevant abstract text.
- Derivative taxonomy remains parent-only where exact nodes were not verified. No positive cultivation edge inferred.
- Loublere/Loubiere discrepancy retained; unresolved PMID lookup does not establish PMID absence.
- Final checks use an attributable local title-markup normalization; original failures retained and fresh-cache portability unresolved.
- Queue retains 241 source-owner candidate links; not exhaustive literature inventory or citation exhaustion.

  summary: Deinococcus radiodurans R1 has an exact strain mapping; the unnamed methionine-synthase
    deletion mutant remains species-parent-only. Actual NCBI ranks retained. Notes
    do not promote unresolved exact-recipe associations into native growth.

  summary: Only bibliography/qualified notes and one history event added. All prior
    fields and original bytes recoverable. No parent/child reassignment, graph edges
    or pages published.

limitations:
- Partial scientific self-review; all 383 amounts enumerated but not newly certified.
- Available sources are abstracts, not full articles. Organism inventory is limited
  to inspected medium-relevant abstract text.
- Derivative taxonomy remains parent-only where exact nodes were not verified. No
  positive cultivation edge inferred.
- Loublere/Loubiere discrepancy retained; unresolved PMID lookup does not establish
  PMID absence.
- Final checks use an attributable local title-markup normalization; original failures
  retained and fresh-cache portability unresolved.
- Queue retains 241 source-owner candidate links; not exhaustive literature inventory
  or citation exhaustion.
